Well, um... I wouldn't say I've been unusually tired, exactly. I mean, at my age, you know, you can't expect to have the energy of a young man anymore. I try to stay busy when I can, and I certainly don't want to come across as someone who just sits around all day doing nothing. 

I suppose maybe I've been taking a few more rests than usual, but nothing too out of the ordinary, I don't think. I really try to push through it so I'm not a bother to my wife. I don't want her worrying about me being lethargic or anything like that. 

I hope that's an okay answer. I just don't think I've noticed a major drop in my energy, all things considered. Is that what you were looking for?

Same with phone numbers, if someone tells me one, I can still hold onto it long enough to jot it down. \n\nI really hope I'm not making things harder for you by saying no to that. I am fighting it, I promise. But... yes, the heavy feeling is there most of the day, almost every day since she passed.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Well, um... I really hate to admit this, because my wife goes to the trouble of making wonderful meals and I'd hate for you to think I'm ungrateful. I really do try to eat, I promise. But... I suppose food has mostly just lost its appeal for me lately. 

I don't want you to think I'm doing it on purpose, but I end up just sort of... pushing my breakfast around the plate. And then, well, by the time supper rolls around, my wife is usually reminding me that I've barely eaten anything all day. I feel terrible making her worry about it, and I'm really trying to do better for her. 

I hope it's okay that I explained it that way? Does that answer your question, Doctor?
